公司簡介


Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the “Company”                 舜 宇 光 學 科 技（ 集 團 ）有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」或
or “Sunny Optical Technology”, together with its subsidiaries, the              「舜宇光學科技」，連同其附屬公司「本集團」）
“Group”) (Stock Code: 2382.HK) is a global leading integrated optical           （股份代號：2382.HK)為一間全球領先的綜合
components and products manufacturer. The Group is principally                  光學零件及產品生產商。本集團主要從事設
engaged in the design, research and development (the “R&D”),                    計、研究與開發（「研發」）、生產及銷售光學及
manufacture and sales of optical and optical-related products. Such             光學相關產品。該等產品包括光學零件（例如
products include optical components (such as vehicle lens sets,                 車載鏡頭、車載激光雷達（「激光雷達」）光學部
optical parts of vehicle light detection and ranging (“LiDAR”), extended        件、擴展現實（「XR」）感知及交互鏡頭、手機
reality (“XR”) perception and interaction lens sets, handset lens sets,         鏡頭、玻璃球面和非球面鏡片及其他光學零部
glass spherical and aspherical lenses and other optical components)             件）
                                                                                 （「光學零件」）、光電產品（例如車載模組、
(the “Optical Components”), optoelectronic products (such as vehicle            XR視覺模組、手機攝像模組、機器人視覺模塊

MARKET REVIEW                                                                   市場回顧
Looking back to the first half of 2024, it was observed that the global          回顧二零二四年上半年，全球經濟增長趨於穩
economy tended to maintain a stable growth, although the growth rate             定，不過增長速度仍低於疫情前水平。地緣政
was still below the level before the pandemic. Geopolitical conflicts,           治衝突、通脹壓力和國際貿易不振等因素持續
inflationary pressures, sluggish international trade and other factors           拖累全球經濟的復甦。然而，中華人民共和國
continued to drag the recovery of the global economy. However, the              （「中國」）的經濟運行總體平穩，轉型升級穩步
economy of the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC” or “China”)                 推進。

transformation and upgrading.

In order to minimise the credit risk in relation to trade receivables, the       為盡量減低有關貿易應收款項的信貸風險，本
management of the Company (the “Management”) has delegated a                     公司管理層（「管理層」）已委派專責隊伍，負責
team responsible for the determination of credit limits, credit approvals        釐定信貸限額、審批信貸及其他監察程序，以
and other monitoring procedures to ensure that appropriate follow–               確保採取適當的跟進行動收回逾期債務。本集
up actions are taken to recover overdue debts. The Group has also                團亦已購買有關貿易應收款項的保險。此外，
purchased insurance relating to trade receivables. In addition, the              本集團於每個報告期期末檢討各項貿易債權的
Group reviews the recoverable amount of each individual trade debt at            可回收金額，確保已為不可收回金額計提足夠
the end of each reporting period to ensure that adequate impairment              減值虧損。因此，董事認為本集團的信貸風險
losses are made for irrecoverable amounts. Therefore, the Directors              已大大降低。簡明綜合財務狀況表所示金額已
consider that the Group’s credit risk is significantly reduced. The              扣除信貸虧損，乃管理層根據過往經驗及歷史
amounts presented in the condensed consolidated statement of                     觀察違約率、對當時經濟環境的評估及將於日
financial position are net of allowance for credit losses, estimated by          後收取的現金流量貼現值估計。
the Management based on prior experience and historical observed
default rates, their assessment of the current economic environment
and the discounted cash flows to be received in future.


The Group has no significant concentration of credit risk since its trade        本集團的貿易應收款項分散於大量交易對手及
receivables are dispersed over a large number of counterparties and              客戶，故無重大信貸集中風險。由於本集團大
customers. The credit risk on liquidity is limited because the majority          部分交易對手為獲國際信貸評級機構評定有高
of the counterparties are banks with high credit ratings by international        信貸評級的銀行，故流動資金的信貸風險有限。
credit-rating agencies.

proportion of borrowings in fixed and variable rates and ensure they             的借貸比例並確保其處於合理範圍內。因此，
are within reasonable range. Therefore, any future variations in interest        日後任何利率變化均不會對本集團業績造成任
rates will not have any significant impact on the results of the Group.          何重大影響。


Liquidity Risk                                                                   流動資金風險

The Group manages liquidity risk by maintaining an adequate level                本集團通過持續監察現金流量的預測及實際水
of cash and cash equivalents through continuously monitoring the                 平，並將其與各項金融資產與負債的到期狀況
forecast and actual cash flows and matching them with the maturity               進行匹配，以維持足夠的現金及現金等值項
profiles of financial assets and liabilities.                                    目，從而控制流動資金風險。

           Restricted Share Award Scheme divided by the weighted average                  制性股份而可能發行的股份數目除以已發
           number of Shares in issue for the year ended 31 December 2023 is               行股份的加權平均數目的數據不適用於此。
           not applicable herein.

 Corporate Governance                                                   企業管治

 The Directors recognise the importance of incorporating elements       董事深信本集團的管理架構及內部監控程
 of good corporate governance in the management structure and           序必須具備優良的企業管治元素，方可促
 internal control procedures of the Group so as to achieve effective    成有效問責，實現股東利益最大化。截至
 accountability and to maximise the Shareholders’ benefits. For         二零二四年六月三十日止六個月，本公司
 the six months ended 30 June 2024, the Company complied with           已遵守上市規則附錄C1所載之企業管治守
 all of the mandatory disclosure requirements set out in Part 1 of      則（「企業管治守則」）第一部分所載的所有
 the Corporate Governance Code (the “Corporate Governance               強制披露要求。同時，本公司已應用企業
 Code”) contained in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules. Meanwhile,       管治守則第二部分所載之良好企業管治的
 the Company has applied the principles of good corporate               原則（「管治原則」），並遵照守則條文及遵
 governance (the “Principles”) and complied with the code               守大部分建議最佳常規。本公司會每年檢
 provisions and most of the recommended best practices set              討管治原則的應用情況，且將按照企業管
 out in Part 2 of the Corporate Governance Code. The Company            治的最新進展完善其企業管治常規。
 annually reviews the application of the Principles and will improve
 its corporate governance practices with the reference to the latest
 development of the corporate governance.


 Internal Controls and Risk Management                                  內部監控及風險管理

 The Board acknowledge that the Board is responsible for                董事會知悉其有責任持續監察本集團的內
 monitoring the Group’s internal control system on an on-going          部監控系統並每年檢討其有效性。董事
 basis and reviewing its effectiveness annually. The Board, the         會、本集團內部審計部和管理層則檢討本
 Group’s internal audit department and the Management review            公司以及其附屬公司內部監控系統（特別是
 the effectiveness of the internal control system particularly          財務、營運及合規監控）的有效性。董事會
 financial, operational and compliance controls of the Company          的審核委員會（「審核委員會」）審議本集團
 and its subsidiaries. The audit committee of the Board (the            內部審計部和管理層對本公司內部監控系
 “Audit Committee”) reviews the findings and opinions of the            統有效性的調查結果和意見，並向董事會
 Group’s internal audit department and the Management on the            匯報審議結果。
 effectiveness of the Company’s internal control system, and
 reports to the Board on such reviews.

     the Company maintains sound and effective internal controls to              監控健全有效，可維護股東的投資權益及

     assets safety. The main functions of the internal audit department          能是審核本公司各附屬公司的經營效益、

     the Company, to audit upon resignation of key management                    核本集團內部監控系統的有效性、審閱業
     personnel, to assist the Board in reviewing the effectiveness of            務流程內部監控、審核全面風險管理落實
     the internal control system of the Group, to review internal control        情況、推動反舞弊建設及審核個別項目（如
     of business processes, to audit the implementation of overall risk          關連交易合規性及發出商品審核報告）。董
     management, to promote the construction of anti-malpractice                 事會每年進行本集團內部監控評估，其中
     and to audit individual projects (such as compliance of connected           包括財務、營運合規監控與風險管理職能。
     transactions and audit report of goods in transit). Evaluation
     of the Group’s internal controls covering financial, operational

Scope of Review                                                                  審閱範圍

We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on                 我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審閱委
Review Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial Information                 聘準則第2410 號「由實體獨立核數師審閱中期
Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity” issued by                    財務資料」進行審閱。審閱該等簡明綜合財務
the HKICPA. A review of these condensed consolidated financial                   報表包括主要向負責財務和會計事務的人員作
statements consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons                    出查詢，並應用分析性和其他審閱程序。審閱
responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying                   範圍遠小於根據香港核數準則進行審核的範
analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less           圍，故不能令我們保證我們將知悉於審核中可
in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong                    能發現的所有重大事項。因此，我們不會發表
Standards on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain              審核意見。
assurance that we would become aware of all significant matters that
might be identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an
audit opinion.


Conclusion                                                                       結論

Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that causes               按照我們的審閱，我們並無發現任何事項，令
us to believe that the condensed consolidated financial statements are           我們相信簡明綜合財務報表在各重大方面未根
not prepared, in all material respects, in accordance with HKAS 34.              據香港會計準則第34號編製。

1. GENERAL INFORMATION AND BASIS                                                  1. 概況資料及編製基準
   OF PREPARATION

      The Company was incorporated in the Cayman Islands on 21                    本公司於二零零六年九月二十一日在開曼群島
      September 2006 as an exempted company under the Companies                   根據開曼群島公司法第22 章（一九六一年第三
      Act Chapter 22 (Law 3 of 1961 as consolidated and revised,                  條法例，經綜合及修訂，以前稱為公司法）註
      formerly known as Companies Law) of the Cayman Islands and                  冊成立為獲豁免公司，其股份自二零零七年六
      its shares have been listed on the Stock Exchange of Hong Kong              月十五日起在香港聯合交易所有限公司上市。
      Limited with effect from 15 June 2007.


      The condensed consolidated financial statements have been                   本簡明綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會
      prepared in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34                （「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港會計準則第34
      “Interim Financial Reporting” issued by the Hong Kong Institute             號「中期財務報告 」及香港聯合交易所有限公司
      of Certified Public Accountants (the “HKICPA”) as well as the               證券上市規則的適用披露規定而編製。
      applicable disclosure requirements of the Rules Governing the

      without allocation of corporate items including unallocated other             無攤分企業項目，包括未分配其他收益、

      There were asymmetrical allocations to operating segments                     分部間存在不對稱分配，這是由於本集團
      because the Group allocates interest income, government grants,               在分配利息收入、政府補助金、折舊及攤
      depreciation and amortisation and gain on disposal of property,               銷以及出售物業、機器及設備以及使用權
      plant and equipment and right-of-use assets to each segment                   資產的收益至各分部時，並未向該等分
      without allocating the related cash and cash equivalent, deferred             部分配相關現金及現金等值項目、遞延收
      income, property, plant and equipment and intangible assets to                入、物業、機器及設備以及無形資產。此
      those segments. This is the measure reported to the board of                  乃向董事會報告以作資源分配及表現評估
      directors for the purposes of resource allocation and performance             的基準。
      assessment.


      Inter-segment sales are charged at prevailing market rates.                   分部間銷售按現行市價入賬。


      For the purposes of monitoring segment performances and                       就監察分部表現及在分部間分配資源而
      allocating resources between segments:                                        言：

          reported to the board of directors.                                           未分配資產。

26. BONDS PAYABLE                                                     26. 應付債券
  On 9 January 2023, the Company issued unsecured                        於二零二三年一月九日，本公司根據美國
  sustainability-linked bonds in the amount of USD400 million at         《證券法》S 條例向美國境外專業投資者發
  the rate of 5.95% per annum which will be due by year 2026 to          行於二零二六年到期之 400,000,000 美元
  professional investors outside of the United States in accordance      年利率5.95 厘的無抵押可持續發展掛鈎債
  with Regulation S under the U.S. Securities Act. The issuance has      券。該發行已於二零二三年一月十七日成
  been successfully completed on 17 January 2023 and the listing         功完成，且債券於二零二三年一月十八日
  of the bonds in the Hong Kong Stock Exchange became effective          於香港聯交所上市。
